    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
>           outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

                               hydrated=[], documents={})
    
        class NoSceneImports:
            def find_spec(self, fullname, path=None, target=None):
                if fullname.startswith(('composition', 'remotion', 'astrid.sdk.authoring_render_preview')) or '.scene' in fullname:
                    raise AssertionError('commit imported scene evaluation: ' + fullname)
    
        def no_renderer(*args, **kwargs):
            raise AssertionError('commit evaluated a scene with the renderer off')
    
        import runtime_protocol.service as service_module
        import runtime_protocol.visual_seam as visual_seam
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(sys, 'meta_path', [NoSceneImports(), *sys.meta_path])
        monkeypatch.setattr(service_module, 'evaluate_closure', no_renderer)
        monkeypatch.setattr(visual_seam, 'evaluate_closure', no_renderer)

            value = case.service.get_project_timeline(project, "film")
            return {"revision_id": value["head_revision_id"], "publication_id": value["head_publication_id"]}
    
        def request(commands, base=None):
            base = base or head()
            return {"api_version": 1, "project_id": project, "timeline_id": "film",
                    "base_revision_id": base["revision_id"], "base_publication_id": base["publication_id"],
                    "commands": commands}

            result = case.service.commit_timeline(project, "film", body, idempotency_key=key,
                publisher="timing-editor", invocation_path=route_path(project, "film"), authorize=authorize)
            document = result["data"]["canonical_document"]
            case.documents[document["parent_revision"]["revision_id"]] = document
            return result

        hydrate = agent._hydrate
    
        def observed(port, bundle, calls=(), **kwargs):
            result = hydrate(port, bundle, calls, **kwargs)
            case.hydrated.append(copy.deepcopy(bundle))
            return result

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc
E           runtime_protocol.errors.RuntimeErrorBase: provider executed a different command

                               hydrated=[], documents={})
    
        class NoSceneImports:
            def find_spec(self, fullname, path=None, target=None):
                if fullname.startswith(('composition', 'remotion', 'astrid.sdk.authoring_render_preview')) or '.scene' in fullname:
                    raise AssertionError('commit imported scene evaluation: ' + fullname)
    
        def no_renderer(*args, **kwargs):
            raise AssertionError('commit evaluated a scene with the renderer off')
    
        import runtime_protocol.service as service_module
        import runtime_protocol.visual_seam as visual_seam
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(sys, 'meta_path', [NoSceneImports(), *sys.meta_path])
        monkeypatch.setattr(service_module, 'evaluate_closure', no_renderer)
        monkeypatch.setattr(visual_seam, 'evaluate_closure', no_renderer)
    
        def head():
            value = case.service.get_project_timeline(project, "film")
            return {"revision_id": value["head_revision_id"], "publication_id": value["head_publication_id"]}
    
        def request(commands, base=None):
            base = base or head()
            return {"api_version": 1, "project_id": project, "timeline_id": "film",
                    "base_revision_id": base["revision_id"], "base_publication_id": base["publication_id"],
                    "commands": commands}
    
        def submit(body, key, authorize=lambda: None):
            result = case.service.commit_timeline(project, "film", body, idempotency_key=key,
                publisher="timing-editor", invocation_path=route_path(project, "film"), authorize=authorize)
            document = result["data"]["canonical_document"]
            case.documents[document["parent_revision"]["revision_id"]] = document
            return result
    
        service.register_timeline_commit_provider(agent.create_runtime_commit_service())
        hydrate = agent._hydrate
    
        def observed(port, bundle, calls=(), **kwargs):
            result = hydrate(port, bundle, calls, **kwargs)
            case.hydrated.append(copy.deepcopy(bundle))
            return result

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc
E           runtime_protocol.errors.RuntimeErrorBase: provider executed a different command

                    raise AssertionError('commit imported scene evaluation: ' + fullname)
    
        def no_renderer(*args, **kwargs):
            raise AssertionError('commit evaluated a scene with the renderer off')
    
        import runtime_protocol.service as service_module
        import runtime_protocol.visual_seam as visual_seam
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(sys, 'meta_path', [NoSceneImports(), *sys.meta_path])
        monkeypatch.setattr(service_module, 'evaluate_closure', no_renderer)
        monkeypatch.setattr(visual_seam, 'evaluate_closure', no_renderer)
    
        def head():
            value = case.service.get_project_timeline(project, "film")
            return {"revision_id": value["head_revision_id"], "publication_id": value["head_publication_id"]}
    
        def request(commands, base=None):
            base = base or head()
            return {"api_version": 1, "project_id": project, "timeline_id": "film",
                    "base_revision_id": base["revision_id"], "base_publication_id": base["publication_id"],
                    "commands": commands}
    
        def submit(body, key, authorize=lambda: None):
            result = case.service.commit_timeline(project, "film", body, idempotency_key=key,
                publisher="timing-editor", invocation_path=route_path(project, "film"), authorize=authorize)
            document = result["data"]["canonical_document"]
            case.documents[document["parent_revision"]["revision_id"]] = document
            return result
    
        service.register_timeline_commit_provider(agent.create_runtime_commit_service())
        hydrate = agent._hydrate
    
        def observed(port, bundle, calls=(), **kwargs):
            result = hydrate(port, bundle, calls, **kwargs)
            case.hydrated.append(copy.deepcopy(bundle))
            return result

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc
E           runtime_protocol.errors.RuntimeErrorBase: provider executed a different command

            case.documents[document["parent_revision"]["revision_id"]] = document

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc
E           runtime_protocol.errors.RuntimeErrorBase: provider executed a different command

original_body = {'api_version': 1, 'base_publication_id': 'txn-8113456f11af4fd5b44a4620262f7c14', 'base_revision_id': 'initial-parent'...{'row': {'id': 'initial', 'kind': 'audio'}}, 'op': 'track.create', 'schema_version': 1, 'select': 'parent', ...}], ...}

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc
E           runtime_protocol.errors.RuntimeErrorBase: provider executed a different command

original_body = {'api_version': 1, 'base_publication_id': 'txn-541c00feb66f4c47a3537ef3f8ed2a0f', 'base_revision_id': 'initial-parent'...{'row': {'id': 'initial', 'kind': 'audio'}}, 'op': 'track.create', 'schema_version': 1, 'select': 'parent', ...}], ...}

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc
E           runtime_protocol.errors.RuntimeErrorBase: provider executed a different command

original_body = {'api_version': 1, 'base_publication_id': 'txn-fb100679baf64eeb9fa5df5a22e4352b', 'base_revision_id': 'initial-parent'...{'row': {'id': 'initial', 'kind': 'audio'}}, 'op': 'track.create', 'schema_version': 1, 'select': 'parent', ...}], ...}

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
>           outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
>           outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)

        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure

            self._timeline_commit_started = True

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc

    def _execute_timeline_commit(self, project_id, timeline_id, request, original_body, *,
                                 operation, idempotency_key, publisher, invocation_path, authorize):
        from .timeline_commit import CommitRejected
        from .timeline_commit_port import CommitUnavailable, RequestCommitPort, detached, public_failure
        if not isinstance(publisher, str) or not publisher:
            raise AuthorizationError("authenticated publisher is required")
        idempotency_key = require_idempotency_key(idempotency_key)
        check = self._timeline_authorize(project_id, timeline_id, authorize)
        invocation = {"operation": operation, "path": invocation_path, "body": detached(original_body)}
        request_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json({
            "operation": operation, "path": invocation_path,
            "publisher": publisher, "request": invocation["body"],
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
        context = {"request": detached(request), "invocation": invocation,
                   "request_hash": request_hash, "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                   "publisher": publisher, "realm_id": self.realm["id"]}
        commits, coordinator = self._timeline_commits()
        with self.store._mutex:
            self._timeline_commit_started = True
            provider = self._timeline_commit_provider
        def port_factory(bound_context):
            self._ensure_timeline_commit_baseline(commits, project_id, timeline_id, check)
            return RequestCommitPort(self, commits, bound_context, check)
        try:
            if provider is None:
                replay = commits.lookup(context, authorize=check)
                if replay is not None:
                    return replay
                raise CommitUnavailable()
            outcome = coordinator.execute(context, provider, port_factory, authorize=check)
            if "http_status" in outcome:
                raise public_failure(outcome)
            return outcome
        except CommitRejected as exc:
>           raise public_failure(exc.failure) from exc
